## `AFG-8129-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Vector-Resistance-Assay Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Pipe Cleaning Video Endoscopy & Gravimetric Residue
